agent with a built-in learning loop — it creates skills from experience, improves them during use, nudges itself to persist knowledge, searches its own past conversations, and builds a deepening model of who you are across sessions. Run it on a $5 VPS, a GPU cluster, or serverless infrastructure that costs nearly nothing when idle. It's not tied to your laptop — talk to it from Telegram while it works on a cloud VM.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse any model you want — \u003ca href=\"https://portal.nousresearch.com\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eNous Portal\u003c/a\u003e, OpenRouter, OpenAI, your own endpoint, and \u003ca href=\"https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/integrations/providers\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003emany others\u003c/a\u003e.   <a href="/NousResearch/hermes-agent/blob/main/README.es.md"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/fd0847426473d023cc0540582c550991fd302ec4be0a6610cf7933980f46f120/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f4c616e672d45737061c3b16f6c2d6f72616e67653f7374796c653d666f722d7468652d6261646765" alt="Español" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Lang-Español-orange?style=for-the-badge" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>The self-improving AI agent built by <a href="https://nousresearch.com" rel="nofollow">Nous Research</a>.</strong> It's the only agent with a built-in learning loop — it creates skills from experience, improves them during use, nudges itself to persist knowledge, searches its own past conversations, and builds a deepening model of who you are across sessions. Run it on a $5 VPS, a GPU cluster, or serverless infrastructure that costs nearly nothing when idle. It's not tied to your laptop — talk to it from Telegram while it works on a cloud VM.</p>
<p dir="auto">Use any model you want — <a href="https://portal.nousresearch.com" rel="nofollow">Nous Portal</a>, OpenRouter, OpenAI, your own endpoint, and <a href="https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/integrations/providers" rel="nofollow">many others</a>. Switch with <code>hermes model</code> — no code changes, no lock-in.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<tbody><tr><td><b>A real terminal interface</b></td><td>Full TUI with multiline editing, slash-command autocomplete, conversation history, interrupt-and-redirect, and streaming tool output.</td></tr>
<tr><td><b>Lives where you do</b></td><td>Telegram, Discord, Slack, WhatsApp, Signal, and CLI — all from a single gateway process. Voice memo transcription, cross-platform conversation continuity.</td></tr>
<tr><td><b>A closed learning loop</b></td><td>Agent-curated memory with periodic nudges. Autonomous skill creation after complex tasks. Skills self-improve during use. FTS5 session search with LLM summarization for cross-session recall. <a href="https://github.com/plastic-labs/honcho">Honcho</a> dialectic user modeling. Compatible with the <a href="https://agentskills.io" rel="nofollow">agentskills.io</a> open standard.</td></tr>
<tr><td><b>Scheduled automations</b></td><td>Built-in cron scheduler with delivery to any platform. Daily reports, nightly backups, weekly audits — all in natural language, running unattended.</td></tr>
<tr><td><b>Delegates and parallelizes</b></td><td>Spawn isolated subagents for parallel workstreams. Write Python scripts that call tools via RPC, collapsing multi-step pipelines into zero-context-cost turns.</td></tr>
<tr><td><b>Runs anywhere, not just your laptop</b></td><td>Seven terminal backends — local, Docker, SSH, Singularity, Modal, Daytona, and Vercel Sandbox. Daytona and Modal offer serverless persistence — your agent's environment hibernates when idle and wakes on demand, costing nearly nothing between sessions. Run it on a $5 VPS or a GPU cluster.</td></tr>
<tr><td><b>Research-ready</b></td><td>Batch trajectory generation, trajectory compression for training the next generation of tool-calling models.</td></tr>
</tbody></table></markdown-accessiblity-table>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Install</h2><a id="user-content-quick-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Install" href="#quick-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Linux, macOS, WSL2, Termux</h3><a id="user-content-linux-macos-wsl2-termux" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Linux, macOS, WSL2, Termux" href="#linux-macos-wsl2-termux"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/install.sh | bash"><pre>curl -fsSL https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> bash</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Windows (native, PowerShell)</h3><a id="user-content-windows-native-powershell" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Windows (native, PowerShell)" href="#windows-native-powershell"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Heads up:</strong> Native Windows runs Hermes without WSL — CLI, gateway, TUI, and tools all work natively. If you'd rather use WSL2, the Linux/macOS one-liner above works there too. Found a bug? Please <a href="https://github.com/NousResearch/hermes-agent/issues">file issues</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Run this in PowerShell:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="iex (irm https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/install.ps1)"><pre>iex (irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span><span class="pl-c1">hermes-agent.nousresearch.com</span><span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The installer handles everything: uv, Python 3.11, Node.js, ripgrep, ffmpeg, <strong>and a portable Git Bash</strong> (MinGit, unpacked to <code>%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes\git</code> — no admin required, completely isolated from any system Git install). Hermes uses this bundled Git Bash to run shell commands.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you already have Git installed, the installer detects it and uses that instead. Otherwise a ~45MB MinGit download is all you need — it won't touch or interfere with any system Git.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Android / Termux:</strong> The tested manual path is documented in the <a href="https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/getting-started/termux" rel="nofollow">Termux guide</a>. On Termux, Hermes installs a curated <code>.[termux]</code> extra because the full <code>.[all]</code> extra currently pulls Android-incompatible voice dependencies.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Windows:</strong> Native Windows is fully supported — the PowerShell one-liner above installs everything. If you'd rather use WSL2, the Linux command works there too. Native Windows install lives under <code>%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes</code>; WSL2 installs under <code>~/.hermes</code> as on Linux.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">After installation:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="source ~/.bashrc    # reload shell (or: source ~/.zshrc)
hermes              # start chatting!"><pre><span class="pl-c1">source</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/.bashrc    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> reload shell (or: source ~/.zshrc)</span>
hermes              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> start chatting!</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Troubleshooting</h3><a id="user-content-troubleshooting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Troubleshooting" href="#troubleshooting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Windows Defender or antivirus flags <code>uv.exe</code> as malware</h4><a id="user-content-windows-defender-or-antivirus-flags-uvexe-as-malware" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Windows Defender or antivirus flags uv.exe as malware" href="#windows-defender-or-antivirus-flags-uvexe-as-malware"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If your antivirus (Bitdefender, Windows Defender, etc.) quarantines <code>uv.exe</code> from the Hermes <code>bin</code> folder (<code>%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes\bin\uv.exe</code>), this is a <strong>false positive</strong>. The file is Astral's <code>uv</code> — the Rust Python package manager Hermes bundles to manage its Python environment. ML-based antivirus engines commonly flag unsigned Rust binaries that download and install packages.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>To verify your copy is authentic:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Install GitHub CLI if needed
winget install --id GitHub.cli

# Login to GitHub
gh auth login

# Run verification
$uv = &quot;$env:LOCALAPPDATA\hermes\bin\uv.exe&quot;
$ver = (&amp; $uv --version).Split(' ')[1]
[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol = [Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12
$zip = &quot;$env:TEMP\uv.zip&quot;
Invoke-WebRequest &quot;https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/releases/download/$ver/uv-x86_64-pc-windows-msvc.zip&quot; -OutFile $zip -UseBasicParsing
gh attestation verify $zip --repo astral-sh/uv
Expand-Archive $zip &quot;$env:TEMP\uv_x&quot; -Force
(Get-FileHash &quot;$env:TEMP\uv_x\uv.exe&quot;).Hash -eq (Get-FileHash $uv).Hash"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Install GitHub CLI if needed</span>
winget install <span class="pl-k">--</span>id GitHub.cli

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Login to GitHub</span>
gh auth login

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Run verification</span>
<span class="pl-smi">$uv</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>LOCALAPPDATA</span>\hermes\bin\uv.exe<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">$ver</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> (<span class="pl-k">&amp;</span> <span class="pl-smi">$uv</span> <span class="pl-k">--</span>version).Split(<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span> <span class="pl-pds">'</span></span>)[<span class="pl-c1">1</span>]
[<span class="pl-k">Net.ServicePointManager</span>]::SecurityProtocol <span class="pl-k">=</span> [<span class="pl-k">Net.SecurityProtocolType</span>]::Tls12
<span class="pl-smi">$zip</span> <span class="pl-k">=</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>TEMP</span>\uv.zip<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-c1">Invoke-WebRequest</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/releases/download/<span class="pl-smi">$ver</span>/uv-x86_64-pc-windows-msvc.zip<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>OutFile <span class="pl-smi">$zip</span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>UseBasicParsing
gh attestation verify <span class="pl-smi">$zip</span> <span class="pl-k">--</span>repo astral<span class="pl-k">-</span>sh<span class="pl-k">/</span>uv
<span class="pl-c1">Expand-Archive</span> <span class="pl-smi">$zip</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>TEMP</span>\uv_x<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> <span class="pl-k">-</span>Force
(<span class="pl-c1">Get-FileHash</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">$<span class="pl-c1">env:</span>TEMP</span>\uv_x\uv.exe<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>).Hash <span class="pl-k">-eq</span> (<span class="pl-c1">Get-FileHash</span> <span class="pl-smi">$uv</span>).Hash</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">If attestation says "Verification succeeded" and the last line prints <code>True</code>, you're good.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>To whitelist Hermes:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Windows Defender:</strong> Run PowerShell as Admin → <code>Add-MpPreference -ExclusionPath "$env:LOCALAPPDATA\hermes\bin"</code></li>
<li><strong>Bitdefender:</strong> Add an exception in the Bitdefender console (Protection &gt; Antivirus &gt; Settings &gt; Manage Exceptions)</li>
<li>Whitelist the <strong>folder</strong>, not the file hash — Hermes updates <code>uv</code> and the hash changes every version</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">For more context, see the upstream Astral reports: <a href="https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/issues/13553" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/astral-sh/uv/issues/13553/hovercard">astral-sh/uv#13553</a>, <a href="https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/issues/15011" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/astral-sh/uv/issues/15011/hovercard">astral-sh/uv#15011</a>, <a href="https://github.com/astral-sh/uv/issues/10079" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/astral-sh/uv/issues/10079/hovercard">astral-sh/uv#10079</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Skip the API-key collection — Nous Portal</h2><a id="user-content-skip-the-api-key-collection--nous-portal" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Skip the API-key collection — Nous Portal" href="#skip-the-api-key-collection--nous-portal"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Hermes works with whatever provider you want — that's not changing. But if you'd rather not collect five separate API keys for the model, web search, image generation, TTS, and a cloud browser, <strong><a href="https://portal.nousresearch.com" rel="nofollow">Nous Portal</a></strong> covers all of them under one subscription:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>300+ models</strong> — pick any of them with <code>/model &lt;name&gt;</code></li>
<li><strong>Tool Gateway</strong> — web search (Firecrawl), image generation (FAL), text-to-speech (OpenAI), cloud browser (Browser Use), all routed through your sub. No extra accounts.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">One command from a fresh install:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="hermes setup --portal"><pre>hermes setup --portal</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">That logs you in via OAuth, sets Nous as your provider, and turns on the Tool Gateway. Check what's wired up any time with <code>hermes portal info</code>. Full details on the <a href="https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/user-guide/features/tool-gateway" rel="nofollow">Tool Gateway docs page</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can still bring your own keys per-tool whenever you want — the gateway is per-backend, not all-or-nothing.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI vs Messaging Quick Reference</h2><a id="user-content-cli-vs-messaging-quick-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI vs Messaging Quick Reference" href="#cli-vs-messaging-quick-reference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Hermes has two entry points: start the terminal UI with <code>hermes</code>, or run the gateway and talk to it from Telegram, Discord, Slack, WhatsApp, Signal, or Email. Once you're in a conversation, many slash commands are shared across both interfaces.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Action</th>
<th>CLI</th>
<th>Messaging platforms</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Start chatting</td>
<td><code>hermes</code></td>
<td>Run <code>hermes gateway setup</code> + <code>hermes gateway start</code>, then send the bot a message</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Start fresh conversation</td>
<td><code>/new</code> or <code>/reset</code></td>
<td><code>/new</code> or <code>/reset</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Change model</td>
<td><code>/model [provider:model]</code></td>
<td><code>/model [provider:model]</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Set a personality</td>
<td><code>/personality [name]</code></td>
<td><code>/personality [name]</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Retry or undo the last turn</td>
<td><code>/retry</code>, <code>/undo</code></td>
<td><code>/retry</code>, <code>/undo</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Compress context / check usage</td>
<td><code>/compress</code>, <code>/usage</code>, <code>/insights [--days N]</code></td>
<td><code>/compress</code>, <code>/usage</code>, <code>/insights [days]</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Browse skills</td>
<td><code>/skills</code> or <code>/&lt;skill-name&gt;</code></td>
<td><code>/&lt;skill-name&gt;</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Interrupt current work</td>
<td><code>Ctrl+C</code> or send a new message</td>
<td><code>/stop</code> or send a new message</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Platform-specific status</td>
<td><code>/platforms</code></td>
<td><code>/status</code>, <code>/sethome</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">For the full command lists, see the <a href="https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/user-guide/cli" rel="nofollow">CLI guide</a> and the <a href="https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/user-guide/messaging" rel="nofollow">Messaging Gateway guide</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Migrating from OpenClaw</h2><a id="user-content-migrating-from-openclaw" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Migrating from OpenClaw" href="#migrating-from-openclaw"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you're coming from OpenClaw, Hermes can automatically import your settings, memories, skills, and API keys.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>During first-time setup:</strong> The setup wizard (<code>hermes setup</code>) automatically detects <code>~/.openclaw</code> and offers to migrate before configuration begins.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Anytime after install:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="hermes claw migrate              # Interactive migration (full preset)
hermes claw migrate --dry-run    # Preview what would be migrated
hermes claw migrate --preset user-data   # Migrate without secrets
hermes claw migrate --overwrite  # Overwrite existing conflicts"><pre>hermes claw migrate              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Interactive migration (full preset)</span>
hermes claw migrate --dry-run    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Preview what would be migrated</span>
hermes claw migrate --preset user-data   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Migrate without secrets</span>
hermes claw migrate --overwrite  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Overwrite existing conflicts</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">What gets imported:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>SOUL.md</strong> — persona file</li>
<li><strong>Memories</strong> — MEMORY.md and USER.md entries</li>
<li><strong>Skills</strong> — user-created skills → <code>~/.hermes/skills/openclaw-imports/</code></li>
<li><strong>Command allowlist</strong> — approval patterns</li>
<li><strong>Messaging settings</strong> — platform configs, allowed users, working directory</li>
<li><strong>API keys</strong> — allowlisted secrets (Telegram, OpenRouter, OpenAI, Anthropic, ElevenLabs)</li>
<li><strong>TTS assets</strong> — workspace audio files</li>
<li><strong>Workspace instructions</strong> — AGENTS.md (with <code>--workspace-target</code>)</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">See <code>hermes claw migrate --help</code> for all options, or use the <code>openclaw-migration</code> skill for an interactive agent-guided migration with dry-run previews.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We welcome contributions! See the <a href="https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/docs/developer-guide/contributing" rel="nofollow">Contributing Guide</a> for development setup, code style, and PR process.</p>
<p dir="auto">Quick start for contributors — use the standard installer, then work from the
full git checkout it creates at <code>$HERMES_HOME/hermes-agent</code> (usually
<code>~/.hermes/hermes-agent</code>). This matches the layout used by <code>hermes update</code>, the
managed venv, lazy dependencies, gateway, and docs tooling.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/install.sh | bash
cd &quot;${HERMES_HOME:-$HOME/.hermes}/hermes-agent&quot;
uv pip install -e &quot;.[all,dev]&quot;
scripts/run_tests.sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://hermes-agent.nousresearch.com/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> bash
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span><span class="pl-smi">${HERMES_HOME<span class="pl-k">:-</span><span class="pl-smi">$HOME</span><span class="pl-k">/</span>.hermes}</span>/hermes-agent<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
uv pip install -e <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>.[all,dev]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
scripts/run_tests.sh</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Manual clone fallback (for throwaway clones/CI where you intentionally do not
want the managed install layout):</p>
<p dir="auto">Create the venv outside the cloned source tree — a venv inside the directory
the agent operates from can be wiped by a relative-path command the agent runs
against its own checkout, destroying the running runtime mid-session.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh | sh
uv venv ~/.hermes/venvs/hermes-dev --python 3.11
source ~/.hermes/venvs/hermes-dev/bin/activate
uv pip install -e &quot;.[all,dev]&quot;
scripts/run_tests.sh"><pre>curl -LsSf https://astral.sh/uv/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh
uv venv <span class="pl-k">~</span>/.hermes/venvs/hermes-dev --python 3.11
<span class="pl-c1">source</span> <span class="pl-k">~</span>/.hermes/venvs/hermes-dev/bin/activate
uv pip install -e <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>.[all,dev]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
scripts/run_tests.sh</pre></div>
